     Business review                                                       業務回顧
     In 2024, the medical industry faced significant challenges such       2024 年 醫 療 行 業 面 臨 區 域 競 爭 加 劇、行 業
     as intensifying regional competition, accelerating industry           加速分化、醫保支付改革等重大挑戰，本集
     differentiation and the reform of medical insurance payment.          團堅持「加快結構改革，持續效益提升」的年
     Adhering to the annual management theme of “expediting                度管理主題，緊抓核心能力建設，打造具有
     structural reforms and continuously improving efficiency”,            華潤特色的醫療運營模式，力求在不斷變化
     the Group vigorously strengthened the building of its core            的行業競爭格局中行穩致遠。
     capabilities and created a medical operation model with the
     characteristics of China Resources, in a bid to strive for lasting
     stability amidst ever-changing competitive landscape of the
     industry.

     During the Reporting Period, the Group’s member hospitals             報告期內，本集團旗下成員醫院積極研判風
     actively assessed and analyzed risks and challenges to                險挑戰，克服醫保支付改革、地區競爭加劇
     overcome the impacts of external environment, such as the             等外部環境影響，持續提升醫療服務能力，
     reform of medical insurance payment and intensifying regional         不 斷 增 強 自 身 在 區 域 中 的 競 爭 力。2024 年
     competition, and continued to improve their medical service           本集團自有醫院的門診人次及住院人次分別
     capabilities to continuously enhance their competitiveness in         同比增長 1.3% 和 1.0%。
     the region. In 2024, the number of out-patient visits and in-
     patient visits of the Group’s self-owned hospitals increased by
     1.3% and 1.0% year-on-year, respectively.

     The consolidated revenue of the Group for the Reporting Period        本集團報告期共實現綜合營業額人民幣
     amounted to RMB9.855 billion (FY2023: RMB10.108 billion),             9 8 . 5 5 億 元（ 2 0 2 3 年 財 政 年 度 ： 人 民 幣
     and the consolidated net profit of the Group for the Reporting        101.08 億元），而報告期本集團綜合淨利潤
     Period amounted to RMB672 million (FY2023: RMB328 million).           為人民幣 6.72 億元（2023 年財政年度：人民
     Earnings per share of the Company amounted to RMB0.45                 幣 3.28 億元）及每股盈利人民幣 0.45 元（2023
     (FY2023: RMB0.20).                                                    年財政年度：人民幣 0.20 元）。

     PRC. During the Reporting Period, the number of conventional          期內，本集團自有醫院常規診療門診量和住

     Exposure to Fluctuation in Exchange Rates and                      匯率波動風險及其他風險

Dividend Policy                                                     股息政策
According to the dividend policy of the Group, subject to           本集團之股息政策，乃在本集團盈利且不影

recorded net profit during the period and payment of dividend       度股息形式與股東分享溢利，派息後的利潤
would not affect its normal operation. The Company intends          餘額將用於本集團之發展與經營。
to share its profits with Shareholders in the form of annual
dividend and the remaining net profits will be used for the
Group’s development and operations.

among other things, the general financial condition of the Group,   集團之一般財務狀況、現時與未來之經營、

by the Company is also subject to any restrictions under the        限制所規限。
Cayman Islands laws and the Articles of Association.

adopted and makes judgements and estimates that are                  斷和估計。
reasonable and prudent in accordance with the applicable

and the Group’s accounting policies. They are also responsible       並因而採取合理步驟防止及查找本集團內的

                                                          •   我們在內部估值專家協助下，抽樣評估 (i) 參考當
                                                              前會計準則規定所採納的方法；及 (ii) 使用價值下
                                                              貼現現金流量預測所用的貼現率，方法為根據外
                                                              部可得資料作出獨立評估；

                                                          •   evaluating the sensitivity analysis prepared by
                                                              management for each of the key assumptions,
                                                              including the revenue growth rates, gross profit
                                                              margins and discount rates adopted in the
                                                              discounted cash flow forecasts under value in
                                                              use and considering the possibility of error or
                                                              management bias;
                                                          •   評估管理層就每項主要假設（包括使用價值下貼

      1. CORPORATE INFORMATION                                            1. 公司資料
           China Resources Medical Holdings Company Limited is              華潤醫療控股有限公司於開曼群島
           a limited liability company incorporated in the Cayman           註 冊 成 立 為 有 限 責 任 公 司。本 公 司
           Islands. The address of the registered office of the             註 冊 辦 事 處 地 址 為 4th Floor, Harbour
           Company is 4th Floor, Harbour Place, 103 South Church            Place, 103 South Church Street, PO
           Street, PO Box 10240, Grand Cayman KY1-1002, Cayman              Box 10240, Grand Cayman KY1-1002,
           Islands. The principal place of business of the Company in       Cayman Islands，於 中 華 人 民 共 和 國
           Chinese Mainland is located at 14/F, Kunlun Center Office       （「中國」）北京市豐台區福宜街 9 號院昆
           Building, No. 9 Fuyi Street, Fengtai District, Beijing, the      侖中心寫字樓 14 樓。
           People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).

           As at the end of the reporting period, the Group’s               於 報 告 期 末，本 集 團 的 直 接 控 股 公 司
           immediate holding company is CRH (Medical) Limited, a            為華潤集團（醫療）有限公司（一間於英
           company incorporated in the British Virgin Islands, and the      屬維爾京群島註冊成立的公司），最終
           ultimate holding company is China Resources Company              控 股 公 司 為 中 國 華 潤 有 限 公 司（「中 國
           Limited (“CR Co.”), a state-owned enterprise established         華潤」）（一間於中國成立的國有企業）。

     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a                 聯營公司為本集團於其一般不少
     long term interest of generally not less than 20%                  於 20% 股本投票權中擁有長期權

     position to exercise significant influence. Significant            響 力。重 大 影 響 力 指 的 是 參 與 投
     influence is the power to participate in the financial             資對象的財務和經營決策的權力，
     and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is             但不是控制或共同控制這些決策
     not control or joint control over those policies.                  的權力。

     A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement                     合 營 公 司 指 一 類 共 同 安 排，對 安
     whereby the parties that have joint control of the                 排擁有共同控制權之訂約方據此

     joint venture. Joint control is the contractually agreed           共同控制指對某項安排之訂約協
     sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists                 定 共 同 控 制，共 同 控 制 僅 於 相 關
     only when decisions about the relevant activities                  活動之決定需共享控制權之各方

     control.

   (c) Fair value measurement                                         (c) 公允價值計量
       The Group measures its financial assets at FVTPL                    本集團於各報告期末按公允價值
       at fair value at the end of each reporting period.                  計量以公允價值計量且其變動計
       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell              入 損 益 的 金 融 資 產。公 允 價 值 乃
       an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly              在市場參與者於計量日期進行的
       transaction between market participants at the                      有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉
       measurement date. The fair value measurement                        移 負 債 所 支 付 的 價 格。公 允 價 值
       is based on the presumption that the transaction                    計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉移
       to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place             負債的交易於資產或負債的主要
       either in the principal market for the asset or liability,          市場進行或於未有主要市場的情
       or in the absence of a principal market, in the most                況 下，則 於 資 產 或 負 債 的 最 有 利
       advantageous market for the asset or liability. The                 市 場 進 行。主 要 或 最 有 利 市 場 須
       principal or the most advantageous market must be                   位 於 本 集 團 能 到 達 的 地 方。資 產
       accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset                 或負債的公允價值乃基於市場參
       or a liability is measured using the assumptions that               與者為資產或負債定價所用的假
       market participants would use when pricing the                      設計量（假設市場參與者依照彼等
       asset or liability, assuming that market participants               的最佳經濟利益行事）。
       act in their economic best interest.

   (k) Impairment of financial assets (continued)                   (k) 金融資產減值（續）
       General approach                                                  一般方法

       ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                     預 期 信 貸 虧 損 分 兩 個 階 段 確 認。
       exposures for which there has not been a significant              就自首次確認起未有顯著增加的
       increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs           信貸風險而言，須為未來 12 個月
       are provided for credit losses that result from default           內可能發生違約事件而導致的信
       events that are possible within the next 12 months (a             貸虧損（12 個月預期信貸虧損）計
       12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which               提 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。就 自 初 始 確 認
       there has been a significant increase in credit risk              起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而言，
       since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required           不 論 何 時 發 生 違 約，於 餘 下 風 險
       for credit losses expected over the remaining life                年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計提
       of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the                虧損撥備（全期預期信貸虧損）。
       default (a lifetime ECL).

       At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                於 各 報 告 日 期，本 集 團 評 估 金 融
       the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased           工具的信貸風險自首次確認以來
       significantly since initial recognition. When making              是 否 大 幅 增 加。進 行 評 估 時，本
       the assessment, the Group considers reasonable                    集團會考慮合理及有理據且毋須
       and supportable information that is available without             花費過度成本或精力即可獲得的
       undue cost or effort, including both quantitative                 資 料，包 括 以 本 集 團 過 往 的 經 驗
       and qualitative information and analysis, based on                及有依據的信用評估（包括前瞻性
       the Group’s historical experience and informed                    信息）為基礎的定量和定性資料及
       credit assessment, that includes forward-looking                  分析。
       information.

       The Group considers a financial asset in default                  倘合約付款已逾期 90 日，則本集
       when contractual payments are 90 days past due.                   團 認 為 金 融 資 產 違 約。然 而，在
       However, in certain cases, the Group may also                     若 干 情 況 下，倘 內 部 或 外 部 資 料
       consider a financial asset to be in default when                  顯 示，在 沒 有 計 及 本 集 團 所 持 有
       internal or external information indicates that                   的 任 何 信 貸 提 升 措 施 前，本 集 團
       the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding                  不大可能悉數收到未償還合約款
       contractual amounts in full before taking into account            項，則 本 集 團 亦 可 認 為 金 融 資 產
       any credit enhancements held by the Group. A                      違 約。倘 無 法 合 理 預 期 收 回 合 約
       financial asset is written off when there is no                   現金流量，則撇銷金融資產。
       reasonable expectation of recovering the contractual

   (r) Provisions                                                    (r) 撥備
       A provision is recognised when a present obligation                如因過往事件產生現有責任（法定
       (legal or constructive) has arisen as a result of a                或推定），且資源可能須於日後流
       past event and it is probable that a future outflow of             出 以 履 行 責 任，而 且 該 項 責 任 的
       resources will be required to settle the obligation,               金額能可靠估計時，則確認撥備。
       provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the
       amount of the obligation.

       When the effect of discounting is material, the                    如 貼 現 影 響 屬 重 大，則 就 撥 備 確
       amount recognised for a provision is the present                   認的金額為履行責任預期所需的
       value at the end of the reporting period of the future             未來支出於報告期間結算日的現
       expenditures expected to be required to settle the                 值。已 貼 現 現 值 因 時 間 流 逝 而 產
       obligation. The increase in the discounted present                 生的增幅於損益表確認。
       value amount arising from the passage of time is
       recognised in the statement of profit or loss.

   (s) Income tax                                                    (s) 所得稅
       Income tax comprises current and deferred tax.                     所 得 稅 包 括 即 期 及 遞 延 稅 項。與
       Income tax relating to items recognised outside                    於損益以外確認項目有關的所得
       profit or loss is recognised outside profit or loss,               稅，乃 於 損 益 以 外，在 其 他 全 面
       either in other comprehensive income or directly in                收入或直接於權益確認。
       equity.

       Current tax assets and liabilities are measured at                 即 期 稅 項 資 產 及 負 債，根 據 於 報
       the amount expected to be recovered from or paid                   告期間結算日前已頒佈或實際已
       to the taxation authorities, based on tax rates (and               頒佈的稅率（及稅法），計及本集
       tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                  團 經 營 業 務 所 在 國 家╱司 法 權 區
       enacted by the end of the reporting period, taking                 現 行 詮 釋 及 慣 例，按 預 期 自 稅 務
       into consideration interpretations and practices                   機關退回或付予稅務機關的金額
       prevailing in the countries/jurisdictions in which the             計算。
       Group operates.

       Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method,              遞延稅項乃採用負債法就報告期
       on all temporary differences at the end of the                     間結算日資產及負債的稅基與其

                probable that a significant revenue reversal in the            認的累積收益金額極有可能不會
                amount of cumulative revenue recognised will not               發生重大收益撥回。

                which provides the customer with a significant                 服務而向客戶提供超過一年的重

                to the customer for more than one year, revenue                以 應 收 款 項 的 現 值 計 量，並 採 用
                is measured at the present value of the amount                 合同開始時體現在本集團與客戶
                receivable, discounted using the discount rate                 之間的單獨融資交易中的貼現率
                that would be reflected in a separate financing                進 行 折 現。當 合 同 包 含 為 本 集 團
                transaction between the Group and the customer                 提供超過一年的重大財務利益的
                at contract inception. When the contract contains a            融 資 成 分 時，根 據 合 同 確 認 的 收

                a significant financial benefit for more than one year,        負 債 的 利 息 開 支。對 於 客 戶 付 款
                revenue recognised under the contract includes the             至承諾的商品或服務的轉移之間

  calculate the present value of those cash flows. If the value        位 組 別 的 使 用 價 值 低 於 賬 面 值，管 理

  changes in these assumptions will impact the carrying                休 金 責 任 的 賬 面 值。有 關 主 要 假 設 及
  amount of pension obligations. Details of key assumptions            主要假設可能出現的變動的影響於附
  and impact of possible changes in key assumptions are                註 31 披露。
  disclosed in note 31.

   owners with lease periods of 50 and 60 years, and no                     60 年 的 租 賃 土 地，且 根 據 這 些 土 地 的
   ongoing payments will be made under the terms of these                   租約條款，無後續將付款項。房產租賃
   land leases. Leases of properties generally have lease                   的租賃期限通常為 1 至 9 年，醫療設備
   terms between 1 and 9 years and leases of medical                        租賃的租賃期限通常為 1 至 5 年。概無

           Liquidity risk                                                            流動資金風險
           Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter                  流動資金風險指本集團因資金短缺而
           difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to shortage               難 以 履 行 財 務 責 任 的 風 險。本 集 團 面
           of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity risk arises                   臨的流動資金風險主要來自金融資產
           primarily from mismatches of the maturities of financial                  和 負 債 的 到 期 日 錯 配。本 集 團 通 過 考
           assets and liabilities. The Group monitors its risk to a                  慮金融負債和金融資產的到期日來監
           shortage of funds by considering the maturities of both its               控資金短缺的風險。附註 2.1 說明管理
           financial liabilities and financial assets. Note 2.1 explains             層 管 理 本 集 團 流 動 資 金 需 要，使 其 能
           management’s plan in managing the liquidity needs of the                  應付到期責任的計劃。
           Group to enable it to continue to meet its obligations as
           they fall due.

           The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance between                    本集團的目標是通過使用銀行借款在
           continuity of funding and flexibility through the use of bank             資金的連續性和靈活性之間保持平衡。
           borrowings. The Group aims to maintain sufficient cash                    本集團旨在維持足夠的現金及現金等
           and cash equivalents to meet its liquidity requirements.                  價物，以滿足其流動資金需求。

   Capital Management                                                資本管理
   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management          本集團資金管理的主要目標為確保本
   are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going       集 團 持 續 經 營 能 力，維 持 穩 健 資 本 比
   concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to        率，以支持業務並盡量擴大股東價值。
   support its business and maximise shareholders’ value.

   The Group manages its capital structure and makes                 本 集 團 管 理 資 本 結 構，並 視 乎 經 濟 狀
   adjustments to it in light of changes in economic                 況的變動及相關資產的風險特徵作出
   conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying         調整。為維持或調整資本結構，本集團
   assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the          可 能 調 整 向 股 東 派 付 的 股 息、退 回 股
   Group may adjust the dividend payment to shareholders,            東資金或發行新股份。
   return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.

   The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of          本集團根據有息銀行借款除以總資
   interest-bearing bank borrowings divided by total assets.         產 的 比 例 監 控 其 資 本 結 構。本 集 團 於
   The adjusted gearing ratio of the Group as at 31 December         2024 年 12 月 31 日的經調整槓桿比率為
   2024 was 10.8% (2023: 15.8%).There were no changes in             10.8%（ 2023 年：15.8% ）。截 至 2024
   the Group’s approach to capital management for the years          年 12 月 31 日 及 2023 年 12 月 31 日 止 年
   ended 31 December 2024 and 2023.                                  度，本集團的資本管理方法並無變動。
